- **remotion-opus**: Make premium launch / promo videos (any length; landscape 1920x1080 + portrait 1080x1920, Remotion) with a few API calls instead of an expensive agent loop. Spec mode fills fixed motion engines (3D fly-through, editorial poster, kinetic type); code mode lets an Opus agent write a bespoke scene with its own components.
